After 15 rounds, with a total of 1,997 others eliminated, only eight players remained in the running for the fame and the fortune, the title and the trophy. They entered the feature match area for one final *Aether Revolt*/*Kaladesh* Booster Draft and this is what they had in their hands when they left the draft table.

#### Yusuf Kemal Vefa's Green-White, Top 8 at GP Prague 2017
